+"""One root-staged, UID1000 offline build payload. SOURCE ONLY until admitted.
+No shell, fetch, browser, model, retry, installed-output substitution or daemon.
+The ordinary system unit owns every compiler descendant and the hard lifetime.
+"""
+import errno
+import hashlib
+from html.parser import HTMLParser
+import json
+import math
+import os
+from pathlib import Path
+import select
+import re
+import signal
+import socket
+import stat
+import subprocess
+import sys
+import time
+from urllib.parse import urlsplit, unquote
+
+STAGE = Path('<SOL_WORK>/FRW041-NATIVE-OFFLINE-BUILD-root-010d839888625d6d')
+LIVE = Path('<SOL_WORK>/FRW041-NATIVE-OFFLINE-BUILD-run-010d839888625d6d')
+NONCE = '010d839888625d6d'
+UNIT = 'friday-native-offline-build-' + NONCE + '.service'
+
+
+def require(value, message):
+    if not value:
+        raise RuntimeError(message)
+
+
+def unique_json(data):
+    def pairs(rows):
+        result = {}
+        for key, value in rows:
+            require(key not in result, 'duplicate_json_key')
+            result[key] = value
+        return result
+    return json.loads(data, object_pairs_hook=pairs,
+                      parse_constant=lambda _: require(False, 'nonfinite_json'))
+
+
+def save(name, value):
+    path = LIVE / name
+    fd = os.open(path, os.O_WRONLY | os.O_CREAT | os.O_EXCL | os.O_NOFOLLOW | os.O_CLOEXEC, 0o600)
+    with os.fdopen(fd, 'w') as stream:
+        json.dump(value, stream, separators=(',', ':'))
+        stream.write('\n')
+        stream.flush()
+        os.fsync(stream.fileno())
+
+
+def read_pin(path, pin):
+    path = Path(path)
+    require(path.is_absolute() and '..' not in path.parts, 'clean_input_path')
+    parent = os.open('/', os.O_RDONLY | os.O_DIRECTORY | os.O_NOFOLLOW | os.O_CLOEXEC)
+    try:
+        for part in path.parts[1:-1]:
+            child = os.open(part, os.O_RDONLY | os.O_DIRECTORY | os.O_NOFOLLOW | os.O_CLOEXEC, dir_fd=parent)
+            os.close(parent)
+            parent = child
+            metadata = os.fstat(parent)
+            require(metadata.st_uid in (0, 1000) and not metadata.st_mode & 0o022, 'input_ancestor')
+        fd = os.open(path.name, os.O_RDONLY | os.O_NOFOLLOW | os.O_NONBLOCK | os.O_CLOEXEC, dir_fd=parent)
+        with os.fdopen(fd, 'rb') as stream:
+            a = os.fstat(stream.fileno())
+            require(stat.S_ISREG(a.st_mode) and a.st_nlink == 1 and a.st_uid == 1000
+                    and a.st_size == pin['bytes'] and stat.S_IMODE(a.st_mode) == pin['mode'], 'input_metadata')
+            content = stream.read(a.st_size + 1)
+            b = os.fstat(stream.fileno())
+            c = os.stat(path.name, dir_fd=parent, follow_symlinks=False)
+            key = lambda s: (s.st_dev, s.st_ino, s.st_size, s.st_mtime_ns, s.st_mode, s.st_nlink)
+            require(key(a) == key(b) == key(c) and len(content) == a.st_size
+                    and hashlib.sha256(content).hexdigest() == pin['sha256'], 'input_changed')
+            return content
+    finally:
+        os.close(parent)
+
+
+def clock_valid(clock):
+    now = time.monotonic()
+    require(clock['assignment'] == 'FRW041-NATIVE-OFFLINE-BUILD-RUN' and clock['unit'] == UNIT
+            and clock['nonce'] == NONCE and clock['budget_seconds'] == 1800
+            and clock['native_effects_authorized_by'] == 'Astra'
+            and all(type(clock[k]) in (int, float) and math.isfinite(clock[k])
+                    for k in ('started_monotonic', 'deadline_monotonic'))
+            and clock['deadline_monotonic'] == clock['started_monotonic'] + 1800
+            and clock['started_monotonic'] <= now < clock['deadline_monotonic']
+            and clock['boot_id'] == Path('/proc/sys/kernel/random/boot_id').read_text().strip(), 'original_clock')
+    return clock
+
+
+def boundary_controls(grant):
+    """Real future controls in the same unit before any candidate tool runs."""
+    observed = {}
+    require(os.getuid() == os.geteuid() == os.getgid() == os.getegid() == 1000, 'rootless_payload')
+    status = dict(line.split(':', 1) for line in Path('/proc/self/status').read_text().splitlines())
+    require(all(int(status[key], 16) == 0 for key in ('CapEff', 'CapPrm', 'CapBnd', 'CapAmb'))
+            and int(status['NoNewPrivs']) == 1, 'zero_caps_nnp')
+    require(os.readlink('/proc/self/ns/net') == grant['root_observation']['namespaces']['net']
+            and os.readlink('/proc/self/ns/net') != grant['host_net'], 'different_host_net_namespace')
+    for family in (socket.AF_INET, socket.AF_INET6):
+        try:
+            handle = socket.socket(family, socket.SOCK_STREAM)
+        except OSError as exc:
+            require(exc.errno in (errno.EAFNOSUPPORT, errno.EPERM, errno.EACCES), 'unexpected_ip_refusal')
+            observed[str(family)] = exc.errno
+        else:
+            handle.close()
+            raise RuntimeError('IP_socket_admitted')
+    # Resolution cannot be accepted from local /etc/hosts; invalid DNS name has
+    # no valid local answer. Native packet denial remains proven by AF and netns.
+    try:
+        answer = socket.getaddrinfo('friday-build-denial.invalid', 443)
+    except socket.gaierror as exc:
+        observed['dns_error'] = exc.errno
+    else:
+        raise RuntimeError('unexpected_dns_answer:' + str(answer))
+    for path in ('/run/dbus/system_bus_socket', '<USER_RUNTIME>/bus', '<PRIVATE_TEMP>'):
+        handle = socket.socket(socket.AF_UNIX, socket.SOCK_STREAM)
+        try:
+            handle.connect(path)
+        except OSError as exc:
+            require(exc.errno in (errno.ENOENT, errno.EACCES, errno.EPERM), 'host_socket_uncertainty')
+            observed[path] = exc.errno
+        else:
+            raise RuntimeError('host_control_socket_reached')
+        finally:
+            handle.close()
+    a, b = socket.socketpair()
+    try:
+        os.write(a.fileno(),b'local')
+        require(os.read(b.fileno(),5) == b'local', 'local_compiler_IPC_broken')
+    finally:
+        a.close()
+        b.close()
+    fds = [int(p.name) for p in Path('/proc/self/fd').iterdir()]
+    require(set(fds).issubset({0, 1, 2, 3, 4}), 'ambient_inherited_descriptor')
+    for number in fds:
+        try:
+            target = os.readlink('/proc/self/fd/' + str(number))
+        except FileNotFoundError:
+            continue  # The bounded directory enumeration descriptor is closed.
+        require(number<=3 and not target.startswith('socket:'), 'ambient_descriptor')
+        if number==3:require(target==str(LIVE/'runtime.stdout'), 'wrong_result_descriptor')
+    observed['local_unix_ipc'] = True
+    observed['inherited_network_socket'] = False
+    return observed
+
+
+def prepare(inputs, clock, end):
+    require(time.monotonic()<end,'preparation_original_ceiling')
+    roots = (Path(inputs['build_root']), Path(inputs['joined_root']) / 'agent-browser')
+    copied = []
+    for original, pin in inputs['files'].items():
+        path = Path(original)
+        target = None
+        if path.is_relative_to(roots[1]):
+            target = LIVE / 'source' / path.relative_to(roots[1])
+        elif path.is_relative_to(roots[0] / 'pnpm-store'):
+            target = LIVE / 'pnpm-store' / path.relative_to(roots[0] / 'pnpm-store')
+        # Full old manifests are retained; tools/crates/fonts are read directly
+        # through read-only binds. Verify every manifest entry once, never only
+        # a selected output or a relabelled donor build.
+        require(time.monotonic() < end, 'preparation_cap_exhausted')
+        data = read_pin(path, pin)
+        if target is not None:
+            target.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True, mode=0o700)
+            fd = os.open(target, os.O_WRONLY | os.O_CREAT | os.O_EXCL | os.O_NOFOLLOW, 0o600)
+            with os.fdopen(fd, 'wb') as stream:
+                stream.write(data)
+            target.chmod(0o700 if pin['mode'] & 0o111 else 0o600)
+            copied.append(str(target.relative_to(LIVE)))
+    require(len(copied) > 323 and time.monotonic() < end, 'complete_private_source_store_required')
+    save('prepared.json', {'verified_inputs': len(inputs['files']), 'copied': copied,
+                          'completed_monotonic': time.monotonic(), 'automatic_retry': False})
+
+
+def clean_env(build):
+    prefix = str(build / 'toolchain/bin') + ':' + str(build / 'node/bin') + ':/usr/bin:/bin'
+    return dict(PATH=prefix, LANG='C.UTF-8', HOME=str(LIVE / 'runtime-home'),
+                TMPDIR=str(LIVE / 'runtime-tmp'), XDG_CACHE_HOME=str(LIVE / 'runtime-cache'),
+                XDG_CONFIG_HOME=str(LIVE / 'runtime-config'), CARGO_HOME=str(LIVE / 'cargo-home'),
+                CARGO_TARGET_DIR=str(LIVE / 'target'), CARGO_NET_OFFLINE='true', CARGO_BUILD_JOBS='1',
+                CARGO_INCREMENTAL='0', CARGO_PROFILE_DEV_DEBUG='0', CARGO_PROFILE_TEST_DEBUG='0',
+                RUSTC=str(build / 'toolchain/bin/rustc'), CC='/usr/bin/gcc', CXX='/usr/bin/g++', AR='/usr/bin/ar',
+                RAYON_NUM_THREADS='2', CIRCLE_NODE_TOTAL='2', NEXT_TELEMETRY_DISABLED='1',
+                NODE_OPTIONS='--max-old-space-size=768', NEXT_FONT_GOOGLE_MOCKED_RESPONSES=str(build / 'font-inputs/next-real-font-cache.cjs'))
+

+def phase(name, argv, cap, clock, env, cwd, ceiling=None):
+    started = time.monotonic()
+    end = min(started + cap, clock['deadline_monotonic'] - 300)
+    if ceiling is not None:
+        end = min(end, ceiling)
+    require(started < end, 'original_phase_budget_exhausted')
+    intent = dict(name=name, argv=argv, started_monotonic=started, deadline_monotonic=end,
+                  cap_seconds=cap, original_deadline=clock['deadline_monotonic'], automatic_retry=False)
+    save(name + '-intent.json', intent)
+    output = os.open(LIVE / (name + '.log'), os.O_WRONLY | os.O_CREAT | os.O_EXCL | os.O_NOFOLLOW, 0o600)
+    proc = None
+    try:
+        with os.fdopen(output, 'wb') as stream:
+            proc = subprocess.Popen(argv, cwd=cwd, env=env, stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL,
+                                    stdout=stream, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT, close_fds=True, start_new_session=True)
+            birth = int(Path('/proc', str(proc.pid), 'stat').read_text().rsplit(')', 1)[1].split()[19])
+            save(name + '-handle.json', dict(pid=proc.pid, start_ticks=birth, pgid=proc.pid,
+                                            pid_scope='payload_private_pid_namespace',
+                                            pid_namespace=os.readlink('/proc/self/ns/pid'), **intent))
+            require(time.monotonic() < end, 'phase_admission_late')
+            code = proc.wait(timeout=end - time.monotonic())
+            require(time.monotonic() < end and code == 0, 'phase_failed_or_late:' + name)
+            remaining=[int(p.name) for p in Path('/proc').iterdir() if p.name.isdigit() and int(p.name)!=os.getpid()]
+            require(not remaining,'phase_descendants_not_closed:'+str(remaining))
+            save(name + '-exit.json', dict(returncode=code, parent_reaped=True, completed_monotonic=time.monotonic(), **intent))
+    except BaseException:
+        # Local leader termination is supplemental. Full detached descendants
+        # remain in the one ordinary unit until native group stop is observed.
+        if proc is not None:
+            try:
+                os.killpg(proc.pid, signal.SIGKILL)
+            except ProcessLookupError:
+                pass
+            proc.wait(timeout=max(0.001, min(5, clock['deadline_monotonic'] - time.monotonic())))
+        raise
+
+
+def dashboard_manifest(inputs):
+    root = LIVE / 'source/packages/dashboard/out'
+    require(root.is_dir() and (root / 'index.html').is_file(), 'actual_dashboard_missing')
+    entries = list(root.rglob('*'))
+    require(all(not p.is_symlink() and (p.is_dir() or p.is_file()) for p in entries), 'dashboard_special_or_symlink')
+    assets = [p for p in entries if p.is_file()]
+    require(any(p.suffix == '.js' for p in assets) and any(p.suffix == '.css' for p in assets)
+            and any(p.suffix == '.woff2' for p in assets), 'actual_dashboard_assets_fonts_missing')
+    pins = {str(p.relative_to(root)): hashlib.sha256(p.read_bytes()).hexdigest() for p in assets}
+    font_pins = {pin['sha256'] for name, pin in inputs['files'].items() if '/font-inputs/' in name and name.endswith('.woff2')}
+    for path in assets:
+        if path.suffix == '.woff2':
+            require(path.read_bytes()[:4] == b'wOF2' and pins[str(path.relative_to(root))] in font_pins, 'real_font_bytes_required')
+    class References(HTMLParser):
+        def handle_starttag(self, tag, attrs):
+            for key, value in attrs:
+                if key in ('src', 'href') and value:
+                    url = urlsplit(value)
+                    if url.scheme or url.netloc:
+                        continue
+                    name = unquote(url.path)
+                    if name.endswith(('.js', '.css', '.woff2')):
+                        target = root / name.lstrip('/')
+                        require(target.resolve().is_relative_to(root.resolve()) and target.is_file(), 'missing_export_reference:' + name)
+    for path in assets:
+        if path.suffix=='.css':
+            for match in re.finditer(r'url\(\s*["\']?([^\)"\']+)',path.read_text()):
+                url=urlsplit(match.group(1).strip())
+                if url.scheme=='data':continue
+                require(not url.scheme and not url.netloc,'external_export_asset')
+                name=unquote(url.path)
+                target=(root/name.lstrip('/')) if name.startswith('/') else (path.parent/name)
+                require(target.resolve().is_relative_to(root.resolve()) and target.is_file(),'missing_css_asset:'+name)
+    parser = References()
+    for path in assets:
+        if path.suffix == '.html':
+            parser.feed(path.read_text())
+    # build.rs writes this exact string when dashboard is absent; it cannot count.
+    require(b'Dashboard not built' not in (root / 'index.html').read_bytes(), 'placeholder_refused')
+    save('dashboard-artifacts.json', {'root': str(root), 'sha256': pins, 'actual_build_required': True})
+    return pins
+

+def main():
+    require(len(sys.argv) == 1 and Path(__file__) == STAGE / 'build.py', 'fixed_root_staged_payload')
+    os.umask(0o077)
+    clock = clock_valid(unique_json((LIVE / 'clock.json').read_bytes()))
+    inputs = unique_json((STAGE / 'inputs.json').read_bytes())
+    # FD3 provenance is observed by the original root control before execution.
+    fd = os.open(LIVE / 'runtime.stdout', os.O_WRONLY | os.O_CREAT | os.O_EXCL | os.O_NOFOLLOW, 0o600)
+    if fd != 3:
+        os.dup2(fd, 3)
+        os.close(fd)
+    os.set_inheritable(3, False)
+    with (LIVE / 'identity-ready.fifo').open('wb', buffering=0) as channel:
+        channel.write((NONCE + '\n').encode())
+    with (LIVE / 'identity-grant.fifo').open('rb', buffering=0) as channel:
+        require(select.select([channel], [], [], 25)[0], 'root_identity_grant_missing')
+        require(channel.read(17) == (NONCE + '\n').encode(), 'root_identity_grant_foreign')
+    grant = unique_json((LIVE / 'host-current-invocation.json').read_bytes())
+    preparation_end=min(time.monotonic()+90,clock['deadline_monotonic']-300)
+    require(time.monotonic()<preparation_end,'preparation_original_ceiling')
+    def preparation_expired(*_):raise RuntimeError('preparation_original_cap_exhausted')
+    old=signal.signal(signal.SIGALRM,preparation_expired)
+    signal.setitimer(signal.ITIMER_REAL,preparation_end-time.monotonic())
+    try:
+        controls = boundary_controls(grant)
+        prepare(inputs, clock, preparation_end)
+    finally:
+        signal.setitimer(signal.ITIMER_REAL,0)
+        signal.signal(signal.SIGALRM,old)
+    build = Path(inputs['build_root'])
+    env = clean_env(build)
+    cwd = LIVE / 'source'
+    node = str(build / 'node/bin/node')
+    pnpm = str(build / 'npm/pnpm-9.15.9/bin/pnpm.cjs')
+    # All tool applicability checks are real future execs, never source PASS.
+    versions_end = min(time.monotonic() + 30, clock['deadline_monotonic'] - 300)
+    versions = [('node', [node, '--version'], 'v22.23.2'),
+                ('pnpm', [node, pnpm, '--version'], '9.15.9'),
+                ('rustc', [str(build / 'toolchain/bin/rustc'), '--version'], 'rustc 1.94.0'),
+                ('cargo', [str(build / 'toolchain/bin/cargo'), '--version'], 'cargo 1.94.0')]
+    for name, argv, expected in versions:
+        phase('versions-' + name, argv, 30, clock, env, cwd, versions_end)
+        require((LIVE / ('versions-' + name + '.log')).read_text().strip().startswith(expected), 'tool_version_mismatch:' + name)
+    locks = {name: hashlib.sha256((cwd / name).read_bytes()).hexdigest() for name in ('cli/Cargo.lock', 'pnpm-lock.yaml')}
+    phase('install', [node, pnpm, '--filter', 'dashboard...', 'install', '--offline', '--frozen-lockfile',
+                      '--ignore-scripts', '--store-dir', str(LIVE / 'pnpm-store'),
+                      '--package-import-method', 'copy', '--reporter', 'append-only'], 120, clock, env, cwd)
+    phase('dashboard', [node, pnpm, '--filter', 'dashboard', 'build', '--webpack'], 300, clock, env, cwd)
+    dashboard = dashboard_manifest(inputs)
+    phase('cargo', [str(build / 'toolchain/bin/cargo'), '--config', str(build / 'cargo-offline.toml'),
+                    'build', '--offline', '--locked', '--manifest-path', str(cwd / 'cli/Cargo.toml'),
+                    '--features', 'rust-embed/debug-embed', '-j', '1'], 900, clock, env, cwd)
+    binary = LIVE / 'target/debug/agent-browser'
+    require(all(hashlib.sha256((cwd / name).read_bytes()).hexdigest() == value for name, value in locks.items()), 'source_lock_changed')
+    generated = list((LIVE / 'target/debug/build').glob('agent-browser-*/out/cdp_generated.rs'))
+    require(len(generated) == 1 and generated[0].stat().st_size > 0, 'actual_CDP_generation_missing')
+    require(binary.is_file() and binary.read_bytes()[:4] == b'\x7fELF', 'actual_native_binary_missing')
+    proof = dict(probe='OFFLINE_NATIVE_BUILD_COMPLETE', native_identity=grant['native_identity'],
+                 cgroup='/system.slice/' + UNIT, boundary_controls=controls,
+                 binary_path=str(binary), binary_sha256=hashlib.sha256(binary.read_bytes()).hexdigest(),
+                 locks=locks, generated_CDP={str(generated[0]): hashlib.sha256(generated[0].read_bytes()).hexdigest()},
+                 dashboard=dashboard, build_completed_monotonic=time.monotonic(),
+                 installed_journey_accepted=False, source_task_accepted=False)
+    require(time.monotonic() < clock['deadline_monotonic'] - 55, 'original_build_clock_exhausted')
+    os.write(3, (json.dumps(proof, separators=(',', ':')) + '\n').encode())
+    os.fsync(3)
+    os.close(3)
+
+
+if __name__ == '__main__':
+    try:
+        main()
+    finally:
+        # Notification only. Original host owner still stops/observes entire unit
+        # and rehashes artifacts; this marker never proves cgroup cessation.
+        fd=os.open(LIVE/'payload-finished.fifo',os.O_WRONLY|os.O_NONBLOCK|os.O_NOFOLLOW|os.O_CLOEXEC)
+        try:os.write(fd,(NONCE+'\n').encode())
+        finally:os.close(fd)

+"""Future explicitly admitted native negative-control source; NOT executed.
+Call inside the same reviewed unit with its original owner staying alive.
+The ordinary root stop must close the whole cgroup, including this setsid child.
+No standalone main, daemon, outside-unit dispatch or default build invocation.
+"""
+import math
+import os
+from pathlib import Path
+import signal
+import time
+
+
+def create_owned_detached_control(clock, typed_root_observation):
+    now = time.monotonic()
+    def require(value, why):
+        if not value:raise RuntimeError(why)
+    require(os.getuid() == os.geteuid() == 1000, 'rootless_native_control_required')
+    require(clock.get('native_detached_control_authorized_by') == 'Astra', 'separate_native_control_grant_required')
+    require(type(clock.get('deadline_monotonic')) in (int, float)
+            and math.isfinite(clock['deadline_monotonic']) and now + 60 < clock['deadline_monotonic'], 'original_control_budget')
+    require(clock['boot_id'] == Path('/proc/sys/kernel/random/boot_id').read_text().strip(), 'native_control_boot')
+    ns = os.readlink('/proc/self/ns/pid')
+    require(typed_root_observation['namespaces']['pid'] == ns
+            and typed_root_observation['unit'] == clock['unit']
+            and typed_root_observation['clock_sha256'] == clock['sha256'], 'exact_observed_unit_control')
+    child = os.fork()
+    if child == 0:
+        # A real detached process, not a mocked status or a literal child count.
+        os.setsid()
+        for entry in Path('/proc/self/fd').iterdir():
+            try:
+                fd = int(entry.name)
+                if fd > 2:
+                    os.close(fd)
+            except OSError:
+                pass
+        signal.alarm(60)  # Supplemental fail-closed ceiling, never stop acceptance.
+        signal.pause()
+        os._exit(91)
+    birth = int(Path('/proc', str(child), 'stat').read_text().rsplit(')', 1)[1].split()[19])
+    return dict(pid=child, start_ticks=birth, pid_namespace=ns,
+                namespace_scope='same_existing_private_unit',
+                original_deadline_monotonic=clock['deadline_monotonic'],
+                natural_alarm_seconds=60, native_stop_required=True,
+                acceptance='root observes exact host-mapped child birth alive before stop; actual fixed stop before60s, cgroup recursively empty, original births absent and original parent reaped; alarm or namespace-init exit alone cannot PASS')

 def typed_value(properties, key, signature):
-    value = properties.get(key)
-    require(isinstance(value, dict) and set(value) == {'type', 'data'}
-            and value['type'] == signature, 'typed_execution_property_' + key)
-    return value['data']
+    value=properties.get(key)
+    require(isinstance(value,dict) and set(value)=={'type','data'} and value['type']==signature, 'typed_execution_property_'+key)
+    data=value['data']
+    if signature=='b':valid=type(data) is bool
+    elif signature=='s':valid=type(data) is str
+    elif signature in ('u','t'):valid=type(data) is int and 0<=data<2**(32 if signature=='u' else 64)
+    elif signature=='as':valid=type(data) is list and all(type(x) is str for x in data)
+    elif signature=='ay':valid=type(data) is list and all(type(x) is int and 0<=x<=255 for x in data)
+    elif signature=='(bas)':valid=type(data) is list and len(data)==2 and type(data[0]) is bool and type(data[1]) is list and all(type(x) is str for x in data[1])
+    elif signature=='a(ssbt)':valid=type(data) is list and all(type(x) is list and len(x)==4 and type(x[0]) is str and type(x[1]) is str and type(x[2]) is bool and type(x[3]) is int and 0<=x[3]<2**64 for x in data)
+    elif signature=='a(ss)':valid=type(data) is list and all(type(x) is list and len(x)==2 and all(type(y) is str for y in x) for x in data)
+    elif signature=='a(sb)':valid=type(data) is list and all(type(x) is list and len(x)==2 and type(x[0]) is str and type(x[1]) is bool for x in data)
+    else:valid=False
+    require(valid,'typed_data_shape_'+key)
+    return data

+"""Exact existing typed identity and anchored byte-read helpers; source only."""
+import hashlib,json,os,re,stat
+from pathlib import Path
+
+def require(value, message):
+    if not value:
+        raise RuntimeError(message)
+
+def native_identity_argv(unit, bus_owner):
+    require(bool(re.fullmatch(r'friday-native-offline-build-[0-9a-f]{16}\.service', unit)),
+            'native_identity_unit')
+    require(isinstance(bus_owner,str) and re.fullmatch(r':[0-9]+\.[0-9]+',bus_owner),'native_manager_unique_owner')
+    path = '/org/freedesktop/systemd1/unit/' + ''.join(
+        chr(b) if 48 <= b <= 57 or 65 <= b <= 90 or 97 <= b <= 122 else '_%02x' % b
+        for b in unit.encode())
+    # GetAll with an empty interface is supported by systemd's sd-bus server.
+    # One read of the exact running unit; explicit AF_UNIX address needs no
+    # additional environment. "call" applies both no-activation/auth flags.
+    return ['/usr/bin/busctl', '--address=unix:path=/run/dbus/system_bus_socket', '--no-pager',
+            '--json=short', '--auto-start=no', '--allow-interactive-authorization=no',
+            '--timeout=4', 'call', bus_owner, path,
+            'org.freedesktop.DBus.Properties', 'GetAll', 's', '']
+
+def validate_native_identity(evidence, owner, relative, fragment, start, end, pid=None):
+    require(isinstance(evidence, dict), 'native_identity_missing')
+    unit = 'friday-native-offline-build-' + owner['nonce'] + '.service'
+    require(relative == '/system.slice/' + unit and fragment == '/run/systemd/system/' + unit, 'system_manager_contour_required')
+    require(evidence.get('manager_scope') == 'system' and evidence.get('manager_identity') == owner.get('manager_identity')
+            and manager_identity_valid(evidence.get('manager_identity'))
+            and evidence.get('argv') == native_identity_argv(unit, owner['manager_identity']['bus_owner'])
+            and evidence.get('nonce') == owner['nonce']
+            and evidence.get('boot_id') == owner['boot_id']
+            and evidence.get('cgroup') == relative
+            and evidence.get('timeout_seconds') == 5
+            and evidence.get('timeout') is False and type(evidence.get('returncode')) is int
+            and evidence['returncode'] == 0
+            and evidence.get('client_reaped') is True and evidence.get('stderr') == '',
+            'native_identity_transport')
+    observed_pid = evidence.get('pid')
+    require(type(observed_pid) is int and observed_pid > 0
+            and (pid is None or observed_pid == pid)
+            and type(evidence.get('pid_start_time')) is int and evidence['pid_start_time'] > 0,
+            'native_identity_owner_pid')
+    a, b = evidence.get('started_monotonic'), evidence.get('completed_monotonic')
+    require(type(a) in (int, float) and type(b) in (int, float)
+            and owner['started_monotonic'] <= start <= a <= b <= end < owner['deadline_monotonic']
+            and b - a <= 5, 'native_identity_stale_or_late')
+    raw = evidence.get('stdout')
+    require(isinstance(raw, str) and len(raw.encode()) < 1048576, 'native_identity_output')
+    def pairs(items):
+        result = {}
+        for key, value in items:
+            require(key not in result, 'native_identity_duplicate')
+            result[key] = value
+        return result
+    value = json.loads(raw, object_pairs_hook=pairs,
+                       parse_constant=lambda value: require(False, 'native_identity_nonfinite'))
+    require(isinstance(value, dict) and set(value) == {'type', 'data'}
+            and value['type'] == 'a{sv}' and isinstance(value['data'], list)
+            and len(value['data']) == 1 and isinstance(value['data'][0], dict),
+            'native_identity_schema')
+    properties = value['data'][0]
+    expected = {'Id': ('s', unit), 'LoadState': ('s', 'loaded'),
+                'ActiveState': ('s', 'active'), 'SubState': ('s', 'running'),
+                'MainPID': ('u', observed_pid), 'ControlPID': ('u', 0),
+                'ControlGroup': ('s', relative), 'FragmentPath': ('s', fragment),
+                'Transient': ('b', False), 'NeedDaemonReload': ('b', False),
+                'DropInPaths': ('as', [])}
+    for key, (signature, data) in expected.items():
+        actual = properties.get(key)
+        require(isinstance(actual, dict) and set(actual) == {'type', 'data'}
+                and actual['type'] == signature and type(actual['data']) is type(data)
+                and actual['data'] == data, 'native_identity_property_' + key)
+    invocation = properties.get('InvocationID')
+    require(isinstance(invocation, dict) and set(invocation) == {'type', 'data'}
+            and invocation['type'] == 'ay' and isinstance(invocation['data'], list)
+            and len(invocation['data']) == 16
+            and all(type(x) is int and 0 <= x <= 255 for x in invocation['data'])
+            and any(invocation['data']), 'native_identity_invocation')
+    return bytes(invocation['data']).hex()
+
+def manager_identity_valid(value):
+    require(isinstance(value, dict) and set(value) == {'scope', 'pid', 'start_ticks', 'boot_id', 'bus_owner', 'uid'}
+            and value['scope'] == 'system' and type(value['pid']) is int and value['pid'] == 1
+            and type(value['uid']) is int and value['uid'] == 0
+            and type(value['start_ticks']) is int and value['start_ticks'] > 0
+            and bool(re.fullmatch(r'[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}', value['boot_id']))
+            and bool(re.fullmatch(r':[0-9]+\.[0-9]+', value['bus_owner'])), 'manager_identity')
+    return True
+
+def start_time(proc):
+    return int((proc / 'stat').read_text().rsplit(')', 1)[1].split()[19])
+
+def safe_parent(path, owner):
+    """Anchor every component; never resolve a mutable symlink or FIFO."""
+    path=Path(path)
+    require(path.is_absolute() and '..' not in path.parts, 'absolute_clean_path_required')
+    fd=os.open('/',os.O_RDONLY|os.O_DIRECTORY|os.O_NOFOLLOW|os.O_CLOEXEC)
+    identities=[]
+    try:
+        for part in path.parts[1:-1]:
+            nxt=os.open(part,os.O_RDONLY|os.O_DIRECTORY|os.O_NOFOLLOW|os.O_NONBLOCK|os.O_CLOEXEC,dir_fd=fd)
+            os.close(fd);fd=nxt;s=os.fstat(fd)
+            require(stat.S_ISDIR(s.st_mode) and s.st_uid in (0,owner) and not s.st_mode&0o022,
+                    'unsafe_input_ancestor')
+            identities.append((s.st_dev,s.st_ino))
+        return fd,path.name,identities
+    except BaseException:
+        os.close(fd);raise
+
+def read_exact(path, uid, mode=None, maximum=1048576, single_link=True):
+    parent,leaf,ancestors=safe_parent(path,uid)
+    fd=None
+    try:
+        fd=os.open(leaf,os.O_RDONLY|os.O_NONBLOCK|os.O_NOFOLLOW|os.O_CLOEXEC,dir_fd=parent)
+        a=os.fstat(fd)
+        require(stat.S_ISREG(a.st_mode) and a.st_uid==uid and (uid!=0 or a.st_gid==0)
+                and (not single_link or a.st_nlink==1) and not a.st_mode&0o022
+                and a.st_size<=maximum and (mode is None or stat.S_IMODE(a.st_mode)==mode), 'input_owner_mode_type')
+        chunks=[];remaining=a.st_size+1
+        while remaining:
+            block=os.read(fd,min(65536,remaining))
+            if not block:break
+            chunks.append(block);remaining-=len(block)
+        content=b''.join(chunks);b=os.fstat(fd);c=os.stat(leaf,dir_fd=parent,follow_symlinks=False)
+        key=lambda x:(x.st_dev,x.st_ino,x.st_size,x.st_mtime_ns,x.st_mode,x.st_uid,x.st_gid,x.st_nlink)
+        require(key(a)==key(b)==key(c) and len(content)==a.st_size, 'input_changed_or_overflow')
+        check,_,current=safe_parent(path,uid)
+        os.close(check);require(current==ancestors,'input_ancestor_replaced')
+        return content,a
+    finally:
+        if fd is not None:os.close(fd)
+        os.close(parent)

+"""Future offline source-contract tests. NOT imported/collected/run by Sol.
+Native controls in build.py and the qualification plan remain separate.
+"""
+import copy
+import importlib.util
+import json
+from pathlib import Path
+import time
+import pytest
+
+ROOT = Path(__file__).parent
+
+
+@pytest.fixture
+def modules():
+    import sys
+    sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))
+    try:
+        values = []
+        for name in ('run-live-unit', 'admin-control', 'build'):
+            spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('offline_' + name.replace('-', '_'), ROOT / (name + '.py'))
+            module = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
+            spec.loader.exec_module(module)
+            values.append(module)
+        return values
+    finally:
+        sys.path.remove(str(ROOT))
+
+
+def typed_fixture(runner):
+    live = Path(runner.IDENTITY['live_directory'])
+    fields = {'User': ('s', 'jericho'), 'Group': ('s', 'jericho'), 'SupplementaryGroups': ('as', []),
+              'PrivateUsers': ('b', True), 'PrivateUsersEx': ('s', 'self'), 'PrivateIPC': ('b', True),
+              'PrivateNetwork': ('b', True), 'PrivateMounts': ('b', True), 'PrivatePIDs': ('b', True),
+              'PrivateDevices': ('b', True), 'Delegate': ('b', False), 'ProtectControlGroups': ('b', True), 'ProtectKernelTunables': ('b', True),
+              'NoNewPrivileges': ('b', True), 'EnvironmentFiles': ('a(sb)', []),
+              'RestrictAddressFamilies': ('(bas)', [True, ['AF_UNIX']]),
+              'SystemCallFilter': ('(bas)', [False, ['connect', 'io_uring_enter', 'io_uring_register', 'io_uring_setup', 'sendmmsg', 'sendmsg', 'sendto']]),
+              'SystemCallArchitectures': ('as', ['x86-64']), 'RestrictNamespaces': ('t', 0),
+              'AllowedCPUs': ('ay', [3]),
+              'InaccessiblePaths': ('as', ['/run', '/tmp', '/var', '/mnt', '/media', '/srv', '/dev/mqueue']),
+              'BindPaths': ('a(ssbt)', [[str(live), str(live), False, 0]]),
+              'BindReadOnlyPaths': ('a(ssbt)', [[x, ('<SOL_WORK>/FRW041-NATIVE-OFFLINE-BUILD-root-010d839888625d6d' if x==str(runner.ADMIN_DIRECTORY) else x), False, 0] for x in (
+                  '<SOL_WORK>/FRW041-NATIVE-BROWSER-BUILD-evidence',
+                  '<SOL_WORK>/FRW041-NATIVE-BROWSER-ENTRY-REPAIR-evidence/joined',
+                  str(runner.ADMIN_DIRECTORY))]),
+              'TemporaryFileSystem': ('a(ss)', [['/dev/shm', runner.IPC_TMPFS_OPTIONS]])}
+    unit = runner.IDENTITY['unit']
+    return dict(unit=unit, manager_scope='system', object_path=runner.unit_object_path(unit),
+                type='a{sv}', data=[{key: dict(type=signature, data=value) for key, (signature, value) in fields.items()}])
+
+
+def test_typed_exact_positive(modules):
+    runner = modules[0]
+    runner.validate_execution_properties(typed_fixture(runner), runner.IDENTITY['unit'], ROOT, Path(runner.IDENTITY['live_directory']))
+
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize('key,value', [
+    ('User', 'root'), ('PrivateNetwork', False), ('PrivateUsersEx', 'identity'),
+    ('ProtectControlGroups', False), ('RestrictAddressFamilies', [True, ['AF_UNIX', 'AF_INET']]),
+    ('SystemCallFilter', [False, ['io_uring_enter', 'io_uring_register', 'io_uring_setup']]),
+    ('SystemCallArchitectures', []), ('AllowedCPUs', [255]), ('RestrictNamespaces', 1),
+    ('BindPaths', [['/run', '/run', False, 0]]), ('EnvironmentFiles', [['/etc/environment', False]])])
+def test_typed_negative_refuses_changed_boundary(modules, key, value):
+    runner = modules[0]
+    evidence = typed_fixture(runner)
+    evidence['data'][0][key]['data'] = value
+    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError):
+        runner.validate_execution_properties(evidence, runner.IDENTITY['unit'], ROOT, Path(runner.IDENTITY['live_directory']))
+
+
+def test_typed_bool_integer_is_not_truth(modules):
+    runner = modules[0]
+    evidence = typed_fixture(runner)
+    evidence['data'][0]['PrivateNetwork']['data'] = 1
+    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError):
+        runner.validate_execution_properties(evidence, runner.IDENTITY['unit'], ROOT, Path(runner.IDENTITY['live_directory']))
+
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize('field', ['manager_scope', 'unit', 'object_path'])
+def test_foreign_manager_unit_path(modules, field):
+    runner = modules[0]
+    evidence = typed_fixture(runner)
+    evidence[field] = 'foreign'
+    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError):
+        runner.validate_execution_properties(evidence, runner.IDENTITY['unit'], ROOT, Path(runner.IDENTITY['live_directory']))
+
+
+def exact_clock(module):
+    start = time.monotonic()
+    return dict(unit=module.UNIT, nonce=module.NONCE, assignment='FRW041-NATIVE-OFFLINE-BUILD-RUN',
+                native_effects_authorized_by='Astra', budget_seconds=1800,
+                started_monotonic=start, deadline_monotonic=start + 1800,
+                boot_id=Path('/proc/sys/kernel/random/boot_id').read_text().strip())
+
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize('index', [1, 2])
+def test_original_clock_positive_both_consumers(modules, index):
+    module = modules[index]
+    assert module.clock_valid(exact_clock(module))['budget_seconds'] == 1800
+
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize('field,value', [('budget_seconds', 120), ('deadline_monotonic', float('inf')),
+    ('started_monotonic', True), ('nonce', '3f4901af6bb7a95f'), ('boot_id', 'foreign'),
+    ('assignment', 'FRW041-BROWSER-NATIVE-CONTAINMENT-TRIAL'), ('native_effects_authorized_by', 'Sol')])
+def test_no_old_clock_or_identity_transfer(modules, field, value):
+    for module in modules[1:]:
+        clock = exact_clock(module)
+        clock[field] = value
+        with pytest.raises(RuntimeError):
+            module.clock_valid(clock)
+
+
+def test_clock_cannot_shift_deadline_alone(modules):
+    for module in modules[1:]:
+        clock = exact_clock(module)
+        clock['deadline_monotonic'] += 1
+        with pytest.raises(RuntimeError):
+            module.clock_valid(clock)
+
+
+def test_existing_noarg_controls_no_build_dispatch(modules):
+    admin = modules[1]
+    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError):
+        admin.fixed_argv('bash')
+    assert admin.fixed_argv('start') == ['/usr/bin/systemctl', '--system', '--no-pager', 'start', admin.UNIT]
+
+
+def test_pending_descendant_is_not_cessation(modules):
+    runner = modules[0]
+    unit = runner.IDENTITY['unit']
+    state = dict(Id=unit, FragmentPath='/run/systemd/system/' + unit, Transient='no',
+                 DropInPaths='', ActiveState='inactive', MainPID='0', ControlPID='0', Job='',
+                 InvocationID='', ControlGroup='')
+    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError):
+        runner.cessation(state, unit, Path(state['FragmentPath']), '/system.slice/' + unit, False)
+
+
+def test_wrong_foreign_fragment_is_not_cleanup(modules):
+    runner = modules[0]
+    unit = runner.IDENTITY['unit']
+    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError):
+        runner.cessation(dict(Id=unit, FragmentPath='/foreign'), unit, Path('/run/systemd/system/' + unit), '/system.slice/' + unit, True)
+
+
+def test_no_dashboard_placeholder_or_missing_font(modules, tmp_path, monkeypatch):
+    build = modules[2]
+    monkeypatch.setattr(build, 'LIVE', tmp_path)
+    root = tmp_path / 'source/packages/dashboard/out'
+    root.mkdir(parents=True)
+    (root / 'index.html').write_text('Dashboard not built')
+    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError):
+        build.dashboard_manifest({'files': {}})
+
+
+def test_phase_expired_never_popen(modules, monkeypatch):
+    build = modules[2]
+    def forbidden(*args, **kwargs):
+        pytest.fail('Expired phase reached process creation')
+    monkeypatch.setattr(build.subprocess, 'Popen', forbidden)
+    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError):
+        build.phase('expired', ['never'], 30, {'deadline_monotonic': time.monotonic()}, {}, ROOT)
+
+
+def test_version_shared_ceiling_cannot_reset(modules, monkeypatch):
+    build = modules[2]
+    def forbidden(*args, **kwargs):
+        pytest.fail('Shared version phase ceiling was reset')
+    monkeypatch.setattr(build.subprocess, 'Popen', forbidden)
+    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError):
+        build.phase('expired-version', ['never'], 30, {'deadline_monotonic': time.monotonic() + 1800}, {}, ROOT,
+                    ceiling=time.monotonic() - 1)
